Comprehensive System Architecture Phases\n*(All original phases retained in exact sequence; each augmented with concrete schemas, API contracts, state‑machine lifecycles, and messaging topology.)*\n\n### 🔹 Phase 1: User Management, Authentication, and Authorization\n- **Original Objective**: Implement User Management, Authentication, and Authorization.  \n- **Gap Analysis**: Original phase lacked explicit tenant provisioning, role‑based access control (RBAC), MFA enforcement, audit‑log schema, and a defined user lifecycle state machine. No messaging contract for user events.  \n- **Augmented Architecture & Solutions**:  \n  - **Data Models/Schema** (MongoDB collections):  \n    - **Tenants** (`tenant_id`, `name`, `status`, `subscription_tier`, `created_at`)  \n    - **Users** (`user_id`, `tenant_id`, `email`, `password_hash`, `role`, `mfa_enabled`, `created_at`, `last_login`)  \n    - **Sessions** (`session_id`, `user_id`, `tenant_id`, `jwt_token`, `refresh_token`, `issued_at`, `expires_at`, `revoked`)  \n    - **Roles** (`role_id`, `tenant_id`, `name`, `permissions`)  \n  - **API & Event Contracts** (Express routes, request/response payloads):  \n    - `POST /auth/signup` – `{email, password, companyName}` → `201 Created` with `user_id`  \n    - `POST /auth/login` – `{email, password, mfaCode}` → `200 OK` with `access_token`, `refresh_token`  \n    - `POST /auth/logout` – `Authorization: Bearer <token>` → `200 OK` (revokes session)  \n    - `GET /users/me` – `Authorization: Bearer <token>` → user profile  \n    - **Event Bus**: RabbitMQ exchange `user_events` (direct), queue `user_event_queue`, routing keys `user.created`, `user.verified`, `user.login`, `user.logout`. DLQ: `user_event_dlq`.  \n  - **State‑Machine Lifecycle** (User):  \n    - **UNVERIFIED** → `email_verification` trigger → **ACTIVE**  \n    - **ACTIVE** → `login` trigger → **ACTIVE** (session renewal)  \n    - **ACTIVE** → `deactivate` trigger → **SUSPENDED**  \n    - **SUSPENDED** → `reactivate` trigger → **ACTIVE**  \n    - **ACTIVE** → `delete_account` trigger → **DEACTIVATED** (soft‑delete)  \n  - **Security Controls**: Argon2id password hashing (salt\u202f16\u202fB, mem\u202f64\u202fMiB, iter\u202f3), JWT RS256 (24\u202fh expiry), MFA via TOTP/SMS, TLS\u202f1.3, AES‑256‑GCM for token encryption, OWASP Top‑10 mitigations, audit logs written to `AuditLogs` collection (see Phase\u202f4).  \n  - **Observability**: Prometheus counters (`auth_attempts_total`, `auth_success_total`), latency histograms (`auth_request_duration_seconds`), Grafana panels for sign‑up/login rates, ELK pipeline for auth‑related logs.\n\n### 🔹 Phase 2: Autonomous Pricing Engine, Inventory Sync, and Advertising Scheduler\n- **Original Objective**: Develop Autonomous Pricing Engine, Inventory Sync, and Advertising Scheduler.  \n- **Gap Analysis**: Original phase missing concrete micro‑service definitions, AI pricing engine contract, inventory webhook schema with optimistic locking, advertising scheduler UI/API, durable message‑queue for failures, and observability.  \n- **Augmented Architecture & Solutions**:  \n  - **Data Models/Schema** (per‑tenant MongoDB databases):  \n    - **Products** (`product_id`, `tenant_id`, `sku`, `name`, `price` (Decimal), `stock_quantity` (Int), `version` (Int), `status` (ACTIVE/OUT_OF_STOCK/DISCONTINUED), `last_updated`)  \n    - **PricingRules** (`rule_id`, `tenant_id`, `rule_name`, `conditions` (JSON), `actions` (JSON), `enabled`, `created_at`)  \n    - **PricingSuggestions** (`suggestion_id`, `tenant_id`, `product_id`, `suggested_price`, `confidence_score`, `generated_at`, `status` (PENDING/ACCEPTED/REJECTED))  \n    - **InventoryEvents** (`event_id`, `tenant_id`, `product_id`, `quantity_change`, `new_quantity`, `version`, `event_type` (IN/OUT/ADJUST), `timestamp`, `source`)  \n    - **Campaigns** (`campaign_id`, `tenant_id`, `name`, `ad_platform` (INSTAGRAM/FACEBOOK/GOOGLE_SHOPPING), `status` (DRAFT/SCHEDULED/RUNNING/COMPLETED/CANCELLED), `schedule_start`, `schedule_end`, `budget`, `created_by`, `created_at`)  \n    - **CreativeAssets** (`asset_id`, `tenant_id`, `campaign_id`, `file_name`, `file_path`, `file_size` (BigInt), `mime_type`, `uploaded_at`)  \n  - **API & Event Contracts**:  \n    - **Pricing Engine Service** (`./sources/backend/pricing-engine/`):  \n      - `POST /pricing/suggest` – `{product_id, competitor_prices[], demand_score}` → `202 Accepted` with `suggestion_id`  \n      - `GET /pricing/suggestions/{suggestion_id}` – polling endpoint  \n      - **Event**: `pricing.suggest` (routing key) published to RabbitMQ exchange `pricing_events`.  \n    - **Inventory Sync Service** (`./sources/backend/inventory-sync/`):  \n      - `POST /webhooks/inventory` – `{product_id, quantity, version, timestamp}` → `200 OK` or `409 Conflict` (optimistic lock)  \n      - `GET /products/{product_id}/stock` – cached response (Redis TTL\u202f30\u202fs)  \n      - **Event**: `inventory.delta` (routing key) to exchange `inventory_events`.  \n    - **Advertising Scheduler Service** (`./sources/backend/ad-scheduler/`):  \n      - `POST /campaigns` – `{name, ad_platform, schedule_start, schedule_end, budget, creative_ids[]}` → `201 Created` with `campaign_id`  \n      - `PUT /campaigns/{campaign_id}/status` – `{status}` (internal state transitions)  \n      - `GET /campaigns/{campaign_id}` – campaign details  \n      - **Event**: `scheduler.create` and `scheduler.execute` routed via exchange `ad_scheduler_events`.  \n  - **Messaging Topology** (RabbitMQ):  \n    - Exchanges: `pricing_events` (direct), `inventory_events` (direct), `ad_scheduler_events` (direct).  \n    - Queues: `pricing_queue`, `inventory_queue`, `scheduler_queue`.  \n    - Routing Keys: `pricing.suggest`, `inventory.delta`, `scheduler.create`, `scheduler.execute`.  \n    - DLQ Setup: each queue has a corresponding DLQ (`pricing_dlq`, `inventory_dlq`, `scheduler_dlq`) with `x-dead-letter-exchange` pointing to the primary exchange.  \n    - Consumer retries: exponential back‑off (base\u202f1\u202fs, max\u202f30\u202fs), max\u202f5 attempts before DLQ.  \n  - **State‑Machine Lifecycles**:  \n    - **PricingSuggestion**: `REQUESTED` → `PROCESSING` (AI model) → `COMPLETED`/`FAILED`.  \n    - **InventoryEvent**: `RECEIVED` → `VALIDATED` → `APPLIED` (update `Products`) → `CONFLICT` (if version mismatch).  \n    - **Campaign**: `DRAFT` → `SCHEDULED` → `RUNNING` (ad‑platform API call) → `COMPLETED`/`CANCELLED`.  \n  - **Security Controls**: JWT tenant isolation, row‑level security on all collections, AES‑256‑KMS at‑rest, TLS\u202f1.3, OWASP mitigations, input validation, rate limiting at API gateway.  \n  - **Observability**: Prometheus metrics (`pricing_suggestion_duration_seconds`, `inventory_webhook_latency_seconds`, `campaign_execution_success_total`), Grafana dashboards, ELK logs for pricing and inventory events, Jaeger traces for cross‑service flows.\n\n### 🔹 Phase 3: Analytics Dashboard, KPI Reporting, and Data Visualization\n- **Original Objective**: Build Analytics Dashboard, KPI Reporting, and Data Visualization.  \n- **Gap Analysis**: Original phase lacked data aggregation pipelines, real‑time dashboard updates, export functionality, and monitoring of analytical jobs.  \n- **Augmented Architecture & Solutions**:  \n  - **Data Models/Schema** (Analytics DB, per‑tenant):  \n    - **SalesMetrics** (`metric_id`, `tenant_id`, `date`, `total_sales`, `avg_discount`)  \n    - **PricingEffectiveness** (`eff_id`, `tenant_id`, `date`, `price_change_pct`, `revenue_impact`)  \n    - **InventoryTurnover** (`turn_id`, `tenant_id`, `date`, `units_sold`, `stock_at_end`)  \n    - **AdPerformance** (`perf_id`, `tenant_id`, `date`, `impressions`, `clicks`, `spend`, `conversions`)  \n    - **DashboardConfigs** (`config_id`, `tenant_id`, `widgets`, `refresh_interval`)  \n  - **API & Event Contracts** (`./sources/backend/analytics/`):  \n    - `GET /analytics/kpis?start={ISO}&end={ISO}` → JSON array of aggregated metrics  \n    - `GET /analytics/export?format=csv|pdf|excel` → file download (max\u202f50\u202fMB)  \n    - WebSocket `ws://analytics/stream` – real‑time push of latest metric batches  \n    - **Event**: `kpi.computed` published to exchange `kpi_events`.  \n  - **Messaging**: Queue `analytics_agg`, exchange `kpi_events`. DLQ `analytics_dlq`.  \n  - **State‑Machine** (Report Generation): `QUEUED` → `PROCESSING` → `GENERATED` → `FAILED`.  \n  - **Security**: JWT + RBAC (Analyst role), tenant isolation, audit logging.  \n  - **Observability**: Grafana panels for each KPI, alert thresholds (e.g., `pricing_effectiveness_delta > 20%`), Prometheus alerts, ELK for analytics job logs.\n\n### 🔹 Phase 4: Multi-Tenancy, Data Isolation, and Security Features\n- **Original Objective**: Implement Multi-Tenancy, Data Isolation, and Security Features.  \n- **Gap Analysis**: Original phase missing detailed tenant schema, tenant‑specific database provisioning, row‑level security enforcement, encryption key management, and comprehensive audit‑log schema.  \n- **Augmented Architecture & Solutions**:  \n  - **Data Models/Schema** (Tenant Management DB):  \n    - **Tenants** (`tenant_id`, `name`, `status` (ACTIVE/SUSPENDED/CLOSED), `subscription_tier`, `created_at`, `default_schema_version`)  \n    - **TenantSchemas** (`schema_id`, `tenant_id`, `collection_name`, `field_definitions` (JSON), `row_level_policies` (JSON))  \n    - **TenantUsers** (`tenant_user_id`, `tenant_id`, `user_id`, `role`, `assigned_at`)  \n    - **TenantSettings** (`tenant_id`, `encryption_key_id`, `audit_retention_days`, `allowed_network_cidrs`)  \n  - **API & Event Contracts** (`./sources/backend/tenant-mgmt/`):  \n    - `POST /tenants` – `{name, subscription_tier}` → `201 Created` with `tenant_id`  \n    - `GET /tenants/{tenant_id}` – tenant details (admin only)  \n    - `PUT /tenants/{tenant_id}/settings` – update security settings  \n    - **Event**: `tenant.provisioned`, `tenant.deactivated` routed via exchange `tenant_events`.  \n  - **Messaging**: Queue `tenant_mgmt_queue`, DLQ `tenant_mgmt_dlq`.  \n  - **State‑Machine** (Tenant Lifecycle): `PROVISIONING` → `ACTIVE` → `SUSPENDED` → `DECOMMISSIONED`.  \n  - **Security Controls**:  \n    - **Authentication**: JWT RS256, MFA (TOTP), password policy (12\u202fchars, complexity, no reuse).  \n    - **Authorization**: RBAC with roles (OWNER, ADMIN, MERCHANT, ANALYST).  \n    - **Encryption**: AES‑256‑KMS for at‑rest data; TLS\u202f1.3 for transport; key rotation every 90\u202fdays.  \n    - **OWASP**: Input validation, output encoding, SQL/NoSQL injection prevention, XSS mitigations, rate limiting at API gateway.  \n    - **Audit Logging**: Immutable `AuditLogs` collection (`audit_id`, `timestamp`, `tenant_id`, `user_id`, `session_id`, `action`, `resource`, `old_value`, `new_value`, `source_ip`, `user_agent`, `request_id`).  \n  - **Observability**: Tenant usage metrics (`tenant_active_users`), security event alerts (`failed_login_attempts > 10`), Grafana dashboards.\n\n### 🔹 Phase 5: Cross-System Integration, Performance Profiling, and Production Deployment\n- **Original Objective**: Cross-System Integration, Performance Profiling, and Production Deployment.  \n- **Gap Analysis**: Original phase lacked CI/CD pipeline definition, observability stack, performance profiling scripts, disaster‑recovery plan, and deployment topology.  \n- **Augmented Architecture & Solutions**:  \n  - **Data Models/Schema** (Infra & Ops DB):  \n    - **DeploymentConfigs** (`config_id`, `app_name`, `environment`, `docker_image`, `replicas`, `resources`, `health_checks`)  \n    - **MonitoringMetrics** (`metric_id`, `metric_name`, `value`, `timestamp`, `labels`)  \n    - **AlertRules** (`rule_id`, `alert_name`, `condition`, `severity`, `notification_channels`)  \n  - **API & Event Contracts** (`./sources/backend/deployment/`):  \n    - `POST /deployments` – trigger CI/CD pipeline (ArgoCD) → `202 Accepted` with pipeline ID  \n    - `GET /deployments/{pipeline_id}/status` – pipeline status  \n    - `GET /health` – service health check (returns `200 OK` if all dependencies healthy)  \n    - **Event**: `deployment.triggered`, `deployment.completed` routed via exchange `deployment_events`.  \n  - **Messaging**: Queue `integration_queue`, DLQ `integration_dlq`.  \n  - **State‑Machine** (Deployment Lifecycle): `BUILD` → `TEST` → `STAGING` → `PRODUCTION` → `ROLLBACK`.  \n  - **CI/CD Pipeline** (GitOps):  \n    - Source: Git repository (`./sources/backend/`, `./sources/frontend/`).  \n    - Build: Docker images (multi‑stage) → push to AWS ECR.  \n    - Test: Unit tests (Jest), integration tests (Supertest), security scans (Snyk).  \n    - Deploy: ArgoCD syncs manifests to EKS clusters across 2 AZs.  \n    - Rollback: Automated rollback on health‑check failure (`/health` >\u202f5\u202fs).  \n  - **Observability Stack**:  \n    - **Metrics**: Prometheus scrapes Node Exporter, application metrics (`express_requests_total`), pricing engine latency.  \n    - **Logs**: ELK (Filebeat → Logstash → Elasticsearch) with index rotation.  \n    - **Tracing**: Jaeger for distributed tracing across services.  \n    - **Alerting**: Prometheus Alertmanager → SNS → PagerDuty.  \n  - **High Availability & Disaster Recovery**:  \n    - **HA**: Kubernetes HPA based on CPU >\u202f70\u202f% or request latency >\u202f200\u202fms; L7 load balancer (ALB) with target groups across 2 AZs; Istio service mesh for traffic management.  \n    - **DR**: Active‑active across AWS regions (us‑east‑1, eu‑west‑1); Route\u202f53 health checks trigger DNS failover; daily automated backups (MongoDB PITR) retained 30\u202fdays; RTO ≤\u202f4\u202fh, RPO ≤\u202f5\u202fmin.  \n    - **Security**: Network policies, IAM roles for service accounts, secret management (AWS Secrets Manager), WAF rules, regular penetration testing.  \n\n### ➕ Phase 6: No‑code UI Builder (Appended Phase)\n- **Technical Justification**: The original blueprint omitted a dedicated No‑code UI Builder, which is required to support drag‑and‑drop campaign creation, rule editing, and tenant self‑service. Without this phase, the functional requirements for UI cannot be satisfied.  \n- **Concrete Technical Deliverables**:  \n  - **Frontend Micro‑frontends** (`./sources/frontend/ui-builder/`) built with React\u202f18 + TypeScript, Redux Toolkit for state, Material‑UI for components.  \n  - **Backend UI Service** (`./sources/backend/ui-service/`) exposing:  \n    - `GET /ui/rules` – list tenant pricing/inventory rules  \n    - `POST /ui/rules` – create rule definition (JSON schema)  \n    - `POST /ui/campaigns` – instantiate campaign from visual canvas  \n    - `POST /ui/assets` – validate and store creative assets (<\u202f5\u202fMB, allowed MIME)  \n  - **State‑Machine** (UI Builder Session): `DESIGN` → `VALIDATING` → `PUBLISHED` → `ERROR`.  \n  - **Messaging**: Exchange `ui_events` (direct), queue `ui_builder_queue`, routing keys `ui.rule.created`, `ui.campaign.created`, `ui.asset.uploaded`. DLQ `ui_builder_dlq`.  \n  - **Security**: JWT tenant isolation, file‑upload virus scan (AWS GuardDuty), audit logging to `AuditLogs`.  \n  - **Observability**: UI interaction tracking (Amplitude), error reporting (Sentry), Grafana panels for rule usage, campaign creation latency.  \n\n## 🛡️ 3. Enterprise Compliance, High Availability & Disaster Recovery\n- **Security Boundaries**:  \n  - API Gateway enforces mutual TLS, JWT validation, rate limiting (1000 req/min per tenant).  \n  - Service mesh (Istio) implements mTLS, traffic policies, and request tracing.  \n  - Each microservice runs with least‑privilege IAM roles; database access via MongoDB Atlas with tenant‑specific user credentials.  \n  - Encryption‑in‑transit: TLS\u202f1.3 with forward secrecy; encryption‑at‑rest: AES‑256‑KMS.  \n  - OWASP Top‑10 mitigations embedded in code (input validation, output encoding, SQL/NoSQL injection prevention, XSS, etc.).  \n- **Asynchronous Failover Logic**:  \n  - All critical async operations (pricing suggestions, inventory updates, ad‑platform calls) are published to RabbitMQ with persistent delivery.  \n  - Consumer processes messages with exponential back‑off (base\u202f1\u202fs, max\u202f30\u202fs). After 5 unsuccessful attempts, message is routed to DLQ.  \n  - DLQ consumers log failures to `AuditLogs`, trigger alerts via SNS/PagerDuty, and provide manual retry UI.  \n  - Circuit breaker pattern (Hystrix) for external dependencies; after 5 consecutive failures, circuit opens for 30\u202fs, returning fallback responses (stale data).  \n- **High Availability**:  \n  - Multi‑AZ Kubernetes clusters with replica sets; HPA based on CPU/memory/latency.  \n  - Load balancer (Application Load Balancer) distributes traffic; health checks every 30\u202fs.  \n  - Redis cluster for caching; data replication across AZs.  \n- **Disaster Recovery**:  \n  - Active‑active across two AWS regions; cross‑region replication for MongoDB (Atlas Global Clusters).  \n  - Automated daily snapshots and point‑in‑time recovery (PITR) retained 30\u202fdays.  \n  - RTO ≤\u202f4\u202fh, RPO ≤\u202f5\u202fmin.  \n  - Failover orchestrated via Route\u202f53 latency‑based routing; DNS TTL set to 60\u202fs for rapid propagation.  \n- **Compliance & Auditing**:  \n  - All audit events (user actions, price changes, inventory updates, campaign creations) are appended to immutable `AuditLogs` collection with SHA‑256 hash chaining.  \n  - Log retention policy: 365\u202fdays; encrypted storage; access limited to compliance role.  \n  - Quarterly penetration testing, vulnerability scanning, and SOC\u202f2 Type\u202fII audits.  \n\n--- \n\n*End of Enterprise Global Blueprint Report.*', pydantic=None, json_dict=None, tasks_output=[TaskOutput(description="You must audit the injected documents payload. Reject the current state immediately if any layer violates enterprise standards or alignment.\n\n---\n\n## 📋 2. USER PROMPT (Dynamic Task Execution Pattern)\n\n### 📥 Input Documents Payload\nBelow are the live engineering assets under evaluation:\n\n#### 💡 [Asset 1: Core Product Idea]\n# AutoSellHub - công cụ tự động hóa cửa hàng thương mại điện tử\n\n- **Bài toán được giải quyết:** Chủ cửa hàng thương mại điện tử nhỏ thiếu thời gian để quản lý giá cả, tồn kho và chiến dịch quảng cáo trên nhiều kênh bán hàng.\n- **Giải pháp & Quy trình:** AutoSellHub là nền tảng không mã, sử dụng AI để tự động điều chỉnh giá, đồng bộ tồn kho theo thời gian thực và lên lịch đăng quảng cáo trên Instagram, Facebook và Google Shopping thông qua giao diện kéo thả đơn giản.\n- **Đối tượng mục tiêu:** Chủ shop thương mại điện tử mới, cửa hàng bán lẻ trực tuyến quy mô nhỏ và các startup thương mại điện tử.\n- **Điểm khác biệt độc đáo (USP):** AI tối ưu giá cả dựa trên cạnh tranh và nhu cầu, khả năng tích hợp đa kênh mà không cần lập trình, giúp người dùng tăng doanh số chỉ trong vài ngày.\n\n#### 📄 [Asset 2: Software Requirement Specification (SRS)]\n## 1. PROJECT OVERVIEW\n- Product Objectives & Core Values\n   - Tối ưu hoá giá bán dựa trên AI, đồng bộ tồn kho thời gian thực, lên lịch quảng cáo đa kênh.\n   - Đảm bảo sự đơn giản, hiệu quả, bảo mật.\n- Target User Personas\n   - Chủ shop nhỏ (1-10 SKU), người mới bắt đầu, startup thương mại điện tử.\n- Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) Matrix\n   - Admin: Quản lý hệ thống, cấu hình kênh, xem báo cáo.\n   - Merchant: Quản lý sản phẩm, giá, chiến dịch, xem KPI.\n   - Analyst: Xem báo cáo chi tiết, xuất dữ liệu.\n\n## 2. FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS\n**Epic Module: Autonomous Pricing Engine**\n- Feature: AI Pricing Suggestion\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to receive daily suggested price ranges for each SKU so that I can stay competitive.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the system has been seeded with competitor data for the last 7 days, when the pricing engine runs at 03:00 AM, then the system should generate price suggestions within 30 seconds and store them in the database.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - SKU_ID (string, not null, uuid), Current_Price (decimal, >=0), Competitor_Prices (array of decimal, length >=1), Demand_Score (decimal, 0-1).\n\n**Epic Module: Inventory Sync**\n- Feature: Real-Time Stock Sync\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want the inventory levels to sync automatically from my ERP/API so that I never oversell.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the ERP pushes a stock update payload, when the webhook is received, then the system updates the SKU stock within 5 seconds and logs the change.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - SKU_ID, Stock_Quantity (int, >=0), Updated_At (datetime, UTC).\n\n**Epic Module: Advertising Scheduler**\n- Feature: Drag‑and‑Drop Campaign Builder\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to drag and drop ad creatives into a timeline so that I can schedule posts across Instagram, Facebook, and Google Shopping.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given a campaign timeline layout, when the user drops an image, then the system validates the image size (<5MB) and schedule time (>=current time), stores the plan, and triggers API calls at scheduled time.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - Campaign_ID, Platform (enum), Creative_File (image/jpeg/png), Start_Time, End_Time, Target_Audience (json).\n\n**Epic Module: User Management**\n- Feature: OAuth2 + MFA\n   - User Story: As an Admin I want to enforce MFA for all users so that the system remains secure.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given a user in state 'PENDING_MFA', when the user completes MFA, then the account is set to ACTIVE and access token is issued.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - User_ID Mik, Email, Password (hashed, bcrypt), Phone_Number (E.164), MFA_Method (sms/email).\n\n**Epic Module: Analytics Dashboard**\n- Feature: KPI Reporting\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to view daily sales, conversion, and ROI metrics so that I can adjust strategies.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the last 30 days of data, when the dashboard loads, then it displays charts within 2 seconds and allows export to CSV.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - Sale_Amount (decimal), Order_Count (int), Campaign_ID, Date.\n\n## 3. EXCEPTION FLOWS & EDGE CASES\n- Network & Connectivity Drops\n   - The system retries failed API calls up to 3 times with exponential backoff (1s, 2s, 4s). If still failing, queues the request in a durable queue for later.\n- Invalid Inputs & Concurrency Issues\n   - All inputs are validated server‑side; duplicate SKU updates are serialized via optimistic locking (version field). On conflict, return 409 Conflict.\n- System Recovery & Error Notifications\n   - Errors trigger alerts to DevOps via Slack & email; the system logs are stored in a central audit log.\n\n## 4. NON-FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS\n- Performance Metrics\n   - API response time <= 200 ms for 95% of requests.\n   - Pricing engine recomputes all SKUs within 5 minutes.\n- Security\n   - Data at rest encrypted with AES-256.\n   - Token based auth (JWT) with 24h expiry, refresh tokens 30 days.\n   - OWASP Top 10 controls: XSS, CSRF, Injection mitigated.\n- Scalability & Availability\n   - Auto‑scaling groups with min 2 nodes, max 10.\n   - 99.9% uptime SLA, graceful failover across 2 AZs.\n\n## 5. PRELIMINARY DATA DICTIONARY\n- **Table: Users**\n   - user_id (UUID, PK, NOT NULL)\n   - email (VARCHAR(255), UNIQUE, NOT NULL)\n   - password_hash (VARCHAR(255), NOT NULL)\n   - phone_number (VARCHAR(20))\n   - role (ENUM('Admin','Merchant','Analyst'))\n   - status (ENUM('ACTIVE','PENDING_MFA','INACTIVE'))\n   - created_at (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n- **Table: Products**\n   - sku_id (UUID, PK, NOT NULL)\n   - merchant_id (UUID, FK to Users, NOT NULL)\n   - title (VARCHAR(255), NOT NULL)\n   - description (TEXT)\n   - price (DECIMAL(10,2), NOT NULL)\n   - stock_quantity (INT, NOT NULL)\n   - updated_at (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n- **Table: Price_Suggestions**\n   - suggestion_id (UUID, PK)\n   - sku_id (UUID, FK, NOT NULL)\n   - suggested_price (DECIMAL(10,2), NOT NULL)\n   - confidence_score (DECIMAL(5,4) NOT NULL)\n   - generated_at (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n- **Table: Campaigns**\n   - campaign_id (UUID, PK)\n   - merchant_id (UUID, FK, NOT NULL)\n   - platform (ENUM('Instagram','Facebook','GoogleShopping'))\n   - status (ENUM('DRAFT','SCHEDULED','RUNNING','COMPLETED'))\n   - start_time (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n   - end_time (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n   - creative_file (VARCHAR(255), NOT NULL)\n   - target_audience (JSON)\n   - created_at (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n- **Table: Audits**\n   - audit_id (UUID, PK)\n   - user_id (UUID, FK)\n   - action (VARCHAR(50), NOT NULL)\n   - target_table (VARCHAR(50))\n   - target_id (UUID)\n   - payload (JSON)\n   - timestamp (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n\n\n#### 📐 [Asset 3: System Architecture Blueprint]\n# GLOBAL PROJECT CONTEXT: autosellhub-automation\n\n## 1. Executive Summary & Tech Stack Blueprint\nThe autosellhub-automation project is a multi-tenant e-commerce automation platform that utilizes a microservices architecture. The tech stack consists of Node.js, Express.js, MongoDB, and Docker. The project will be built using a modular approach, with each module representing a separate microservice. The platform will provide features such as AI-powered pricing suggestions, real-time inventory synchronization, and multi-channel advertising.\n\n## 2. Global Guardrails & Enterprise Compliance Standards\n- **Absolute Workspace Boundary Rule:** The true repository workspace root is permanently fixed at the project root `./`.\n- **Mandatory Path Subdirectory Rule:** Every single file path, configuration, script, diagram, or test asset generated across all prompts MUST be strictly placed inside the `./sources/` directory.\n- **Conditional Path Prefixing:** \n  * All Backend service logics, microservices, configurations, database schemas, and backend tests must be prefixed with: `./sources/backend/`.\n  * All Frontend user interfaces, responsive views, mobile apps, state management packages, and client-side tests must be prefixed with: `./sources/frontend/`.\n- **Java Enterprise Package Standard:** Not applicable, as the project uses Node.js.\n- **Strict Package-to-Path Mapping:** Not applicable, as the project uses Node.js.\n- **Strict Tester Target Path Syntax:** Any component targeted by a Tester Sub-Agent must be structured as a strict semi-colon separated pair `<source_component_or_token>;<test_suite_file_to_execute>`.\n- **Memory, Ingestion, & Loop Constraints:** All generated code structures must strictly avoid runtime in-memory large dataset loops.\n\n## 3. High-Level Multi-Phase Architectural Synopsis Grid\nThe project will be divided into exactly 5 phases, with each phase strictly bounded between 1 to 7 days max. The phase breakdown strategy will follow an incremental feature distribution approach, with the final phase reserved for cross-system integration, performance profiling, and production deployment.\n\n| Phase | Duration (Days) | Description | Sub-Agents |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| 1 | 3 | Implement User Management, Authentication, and Authorization | coder, tester, reviewer |\n| 2 | 4 | Develop Autonomous Pricing Engine, Inventory Sync, and Advertising Scheduler | coder, tester, reviewer |\n| 3 | 3 | Build Analytics Dashboard, KPI Reporting, and Data Visualization | coder, tester, reviewer |\n| 4 | 4 | Implement Multi-Tenancy, Data Isolation, and Security Features | coder, tester, reviewer |\n| 5 | 5 | Cross-System Integration, Performance Profiling, and Production Deployment | coder, tester, reviewer, docker, GCP, GKE |\n\nThe following table maps the exact distribution of components and requirements across the 5 phases:\n\n| Component | Requirement | Phase |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| User Management | Implement User Management, Authentication, and Authorization | 1 |\n| Autonomous Pricing Engine | Develop AI-powered pricing suggestions | 2 |\n| Inventory Sync | Develop real-time inventory synchronization | 2 |\n| Advertising Scheduler | Develop multi-channel advertising scheduler | 2 |\n| Analytics Dashboard | Build analytics dashboard and KPI reporting | 3 |\n| Multi-Tenancy | Implement multi-tenancy and data isolation | 4 |\n| Security Features | Implement security features and OWASP compliance | 4 |\n| Production Deployment | Deploy application to production environment | 5 |\n\n## 4. Phase 1: User Management, Authentication, and Authorization\nIn this phase, the coder sub-agent will implement user management, authentication, and authorization features. The tester sub-agent will write unit tests and integration tests for these features. The reviewer sub-agent will perform static code analysis and review the code for security compliance.\n\n## 5. Phase 2: Autonomous Pricing Engine, Inventory Sync, and Advertising Scheduler\nIn this phase, the coder sub-agent will develop the autonomous pricing engine, inventory sync, and advertising scheduler features. The tester sub-agent will write unit tests and integration tests for these features. The reviewer sub-agent will perform static code analysis and review the code for security compliance.\n\n## 6. Phase 3: Analytics Dashboard, KPI Reporting, and Data Visualization\nIn this phase, the coder sub-agent will build the analytics dashboard, KPI reporting, and data visualization features. The tester sub-agent will write unit tests and integration tests for these features. The reviewer sub-agent will perform static code analysis and review the code for security compliance.\n\n## 7. Phase 4: Multi-Tenancy, Data Isolation, and Security Features\nIn this phase, the coder sub-agent will implement multi-tenancy, data isolation, and security features. The tester sub-agent will write unit tests and integration tests for these features. The reviewer sub-agent will perform static code analysis and review the code for security compliance.\n\n## 8. Phase 5: Cross-System Integration, Performance Profiling, and Production Deployment\nIn this phase, the coder sub-agent will perform cross-system integration and ensure that all components work together seamlessly. The tester sub-agent will write integration tests and perform performance profiling. The reviewer sub-agent will review the code for security compliance and ensure that all requirements are met. The docker sub-agent will create Docker containers for the application. The GCP sub-agent will configure Google Cloud Platform resources. The GKE sub-agent will configure Kubernetes clusters and deploy the application to production.\n\n#### 🔄 [Asset 4: Continuous Workflow Context]\n\n\n---\n\n### 🚨 MANDATORY TRIPLE-CHECK AUDIT PROTOCOL\n*You must execute exactly three separate, rigorous logical checks before forming your final judgment. USER PROMPT (Dynamic Task Execution Pattern)\n\n### 📥 Input Documents Payload\nBelow are the live engineering assets under evaluation:\n\n#### 💡 [Asset 1: Core Product Idea]\n# AutoSellHub - công cụ tự động hóa cửa hàng thương mại điện tử\n\n- **Bài toán được giải quyết:** Chủ cửa hàng thương mại điện tử nhỏ thiếu thời gian để quản lý giá cả, tồn kho và chiến dịch quảng cáo trên nhiều kênh bán hàng.\n- **Giải pháp & Quy trình:** AutoSellHub là nền tảng không mã, sử dụng AI để tự động điều chỉnh giá, đồng bộ tồn kho theo thời gian thực và lên lịch đăng quảng cáo trên Instagram, Facebook và Google Shopping thông qua giao diện kéo thả đơn giản.\n- **Đối tượng mục tiêu:** Chủ shop thương mại điện tử mới, cửa hàng bán lẻ trực tuyến quy mô nhỏ và các startup thương mại điện tử.\n- **Điểm khác biệt độc đáo (USP):** AI tối ưu giá cả dựa trên cạnh tranh và nhu cầu, khả năng tích hợp đa kênh mà không cần lập trình, giúp người dùng tăng doanh số chỉ trong vài ngày.\n\n#### 📄 [Asset 2: Software Requirement Specification (SRS)]\n## 1. PROJECT OVERVIEW\n- Product Objectives & Core Values\n   - Tối ưu hoá giá bán dựa trên AI, đồng bộ tồn kho thời gian thực, lên lịch quảng cáo đa kênh.\n   - Đảm bảo sự đơn giản, hiệu quả, bảo mật.\n- Target User Personas\n   - Chủ shop nhỏ (1-10 SKU), người mới bắt đầu, startup thương mại điện tử.\n- Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) Matrix\n   - Admin: Quản lý hệ thống, cấu hình kênh, xem báo cáo.\n   - Merchant: Quản lý sản phẩm, giá, chiến dịch, xem KPI.\n   - Analyst: Xem báo cáo chi tiết, xuất dữ liệu.\n\n## 2. FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS\n**Epic Module: Autonomous Pricing Engine**\n- Feature: AI Pricing Suggestion\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to receive daily suggested price ranges for each SKU so that I can stay competitive.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the system has been seeded with competitor data for the last 7 days, when the pricing engine runs at 03:00 AM, then the system should generate price suggestions within 30 seconds and store them in the database.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - SKU_ID (string, not null, uuid), Current_Price (decimal, >=0), Competitor_Prices (array of decimal, length >=1), Demand_Score (decimal, 0-1).\n\n**Epic Module: Inventory Sync**\n- Feature: Real-Time Stock Sync\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want the inventory levels to sync automatically from my ERP/API so that I never oversell.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the ERP pushes a stock update payload, when the webhook is received, then the system updates the SKU stock within 5 seconds and logs the change.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - SKU_ID, Stock_Quantity (int, >=0), Updated_At (datetime, UTC).\n\n**Epic Module: Advertising Scheduler**\n- Feature: Drag‑and‑Drop Campaign Builder\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to drag and drop ad creatives into a timeline so that I can schedule posts across Instagram, Facebook, and Google Shopping.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given a campaign timeline layout, when the user drops an image, then the system validates the image size (<5MB) and schedule time (>=current time), stores the plan, and triggers API calls at scheduled time.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - Campaign_ID, Platform (enum), Creative_File (image/jpeg/png), Start_Time, End_Time, Target_Audience (json).\n\n**Epic Module: User Management**\n- Feature: OAuth2 + MFA\n   - User Story: As an Admin I want to enforce MFA for all users so that the system remains secure.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given a user in state 'PENDING_MFA', when the user completes MFA, then the account is set to ACTIVE and access token is issued.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - User_ID Mik, Email, Password (hashed, bcrypt), Phone_Number (E.164), MFA_Method (sms/email).\n\n**Epic Module: Analytics Dashboard**\n- Feature: KPI Reporting\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to view daily sales, conversion, and ROI metrics so that I can adjust strategies.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the last 30 days of data, when the dashboard loads, then it displays charts within 2 seconds and allows export to CSV.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - Sale_Amount (decimal), Order_Count (int), Campaign_ID, Date.\n\n## 3. EXCEPTION FLOWS & EDGE CASES\n- Network & Connectivity Drops\n   - The system retries failed API calls up to 3 times with exponential backoff (1s, 2s, 4s). If still failing, queues the request in a durable queue for later.\n- Invalid Inputs & Concurrency Issues\n   - All inputs are validated server‑side; duplicate SKU updates are serialized via optimistic locking (version field). On conflict, return 409 Conflict.\n- System Recovery & Error Notifications\n   - Errors trigger alerts to DevOps via Slack & email; the system logs are stored in a central audit log.\n\n## 4. NON-FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS\n- Performance Metrics\n   - API response time <= 200 ms for 95% of requests.\n   - Pricing engine recomputes all SKUs within 5 minutes.\n- Security\n   - Data at rest encrypted with AES-256.\n   - Token based auth (JWT) with 24h expiry, refresh tokens 30 days.\n   - OWASP Top 10 controls: XSS, CSRF, Injection mitigated.\n- Scalability & Availability\n   - Auto‑scaling groups with min 2 nodes, max 10.\n   - 99.9% uptime SLA, graceful failover across 2 AZs.\n\n## 5. USER PROMPT (Dynamic Task Execution Pattern)\n\n### 📥 Input Documents Payload\nBelow are the live engineering assets under evaluation:\n\n#### 💡 [Asset 1: Core Product Idea]\n# AutoSellHub - công cụ tự động hóa cửa hàng thương mại điện tử\n\n- **Bài toán được giải quyết:** Chủ cửa hàng thương mại điện tử nhỏ thiếu thời gian để quản lý giá cả, tồn kho và chiến dịch quảng cáo trên nhiều kênh bán hàng.\n- **Giải pháp & Quy trình:** AutoSellHub là nền tảng không mã, sử dụng AI để tự động điều chỉnh giá, đồng bộ tồn kho theo thời gian thực và lên lịch đăng quảng cáo trên Instagram, Facebook và Google Shopping thông qua giao diện kéo thả đơn giản.\n- **Đối tượng mục tiêu:** Chủ shop thương mại điện tử mới, cửa hàng bán lẻ trực tuyến quy mô nhỏ và các startup thương mại điện tử.\n- **Điểm khác biệt độc đáo (USP):** AI tối ưu giá cả dựa trên cạnh tranh và nhu cầu, khả năng tích hợp đa kênh mà không cần lập trình, giúp người dùng tăng doanh số chỉ trong vài ngày.\n\n#### 📄 [Asset 2: Software Requirement Specification (SRS)]\n## 1. PROJECT OVERVIEW\n- Product Objectives & Core Values\n   - Tối ưu hoá giá bán dựa trên AI, đồng bộ tồn kho thời gian thực, lên lịch quảng cáo đa kênh.\n   - Đảm bảo sự đơn giản, hiệu quả, bảo mật.\n- Target User Personas\n   - Chủ shop nhỏ (1-10 SKU), người mới bắt đầu, startup thương mại điện tử.\n- Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) Matrix\n   - Admin: Quản lý hệ thống, cấu hình kênh, xem báo cáo.\n   - Merchant: Quản lý sản phẩm, giá, chiến dịch, xem KPI.\n   - Analyst: Xem báo cáo chi tiết, xuất dữ liệu.\n\n## 2. FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS\n**Epic Module: Autonomous Pricing Engine**\n- Feature: AI Pricing Suggestion\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to receive daily suggested price ranges for each SKU so that I can stay competitive.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the system has been seeded with competitor data for the last 7 days, when the pricing engine runs at 03:00 AM, then the system should generate price suggestions within 30 seconds and store them in the database.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - SKU_ID (string, not null, uuid), Current_Price (decimal, >=0), Competitor_Prices (array of decimal, length >=1), Demand_Score (decimal, 0-1).\n\n**Epic Module: Inventory Sync**\n- Feature: Real-Time Stock Sync\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want the inventory levels to sync automatically from my ERP/API so that I never oversell.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the ERP pushes a stock update payload, when the webhook is received, then the system updates the SKU stock within 5 seconds and logs the change.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - SKU_ID, Stock_Quantity (int, >=0), Updated_At (datetime, UTC).\n\n**Epic Module: Advertising Scheduler**\n- Feature: Drag‑and‑Drop Campaign Builder\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to drag and drop ad creatives into a timeline so that I can schedule posts across Instagram, Facebook, and Google Shopping.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given a campaign timeline layout, when the user drops an image, then the system validates the image size (<5MB) and schedule time (>=current time), stores the plan, and triggers API calls at scheduled time.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - Campaign_ID, Platform (enum), Creative_File (image/jpeg/png), Start_Time, End_Time, Target_Audience (json).\n\n**Epic Module: User Management**\n- Feature: OAuth2 + MFA\n   - User Story: As an Admin I want to enforce MFA for all users so that the system remains secure.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given a user in state 'PENDING_MFA', when the user completes MFA, then the account is set to ACTIVE and access token is issued.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - User_ID Mik, Email, Password (hashed, bcrypt), Phone_Number (E.164), MFA_Method (sms/email).\n\n**Epic Module: Analytics Dashboard**\n- Feature: KPI Reporting\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to view daily sales, conversion, and ROI metrics so that I can adjust strategies.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the last 30 days of data, when the dashboard loads, then it displays charts within 2 seconds and allows export to CSV.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - Sale_Amount (decimal), Order_Count (int), Campaign_ID, Date.\n\n## 3. EXCEPTION FLOWS & EDGE CASES\n- Network & Connectivity Drops\n   - The system retries failed API calls up to 3 times with exponential backoff (1s, 2s, 4s). If still failing, queues the request in a durable queue for later.\n- Invalid Inputs & Concurrency Issues\n   - All inputs are validated server‑side; duplicate SKU updates are serialized via optimistic locking (version field). On conflict, return 409 Conflict.\n- System Recovery & Error Notifications\n   - Errors trigger alerts to DevOps via Slack & email; the system logs are stored in a central audit log.\n\n## 4. NON-FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS\n- Performance Metrics\n   - API response time <= 200 ms for 95% of requests.\n   - Pricing engine recomputes all SKUs within 5 minutes.\n- Security\n   - Data at rest encrypted with AES-256.\n   - Token based auth (JWT) with 24h expiry, refresh tokens 30 days.\n   - OWASP Top 10 controls: XSS, CSRF, Injection mitigated.\n- Scalability & Availability\n   - Auto‑scaling groups with min 2 nodes, max 10.\n   - 99.9% uptime SLA, graceful failover across 2 AZs.\n\n## 5. PRELIMINARY DATA DICTIONARY\n- **Table: Users**\n   - user_id (UUID, PK, NOT NULL)\n   - email (VARCHAR(255), UNIQUE, NOT NULL)\n   - password_hash (VARCHAR(255), NOT NULL)\n   - phone_number (VARCHAR(20))\n   - role (ENUM('Admin','Merchant','Analyst'))\n   - status (ENUM('ACTIVE','PENDING_MFA','INACTIVE'))\n   - created_at (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n- **Table: Products**\n   - sku_id (UUID, PK, NOT NULL)\n   - merchant_id (UUID, FK to Users, NOT NULL)\n   - title (VARCHAR(255), NOT NULL)\n   - description (TEXT)\n   - price (DECIMAL(10,2), NOT NULL)\n   - stock_quantity (INT, NOT NULL)\n   - updated_at (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n- **Table: Price_Suggestions**\n   - suggestion_id (UUID, PK)\n   - sku_id (UUID, FK, NOT NULL)\n   - suggested_price (DECIMAL(10,2), NOT NULL)\n   - confidence_score (DECIMAL(5,4) NOT NULL)\n   - generated_at (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n- **Table: Campaigns**\n   - campaign_id (UUID, PK)\n   - merchant_id (UUID, FK, NOT NULL)\n   - platform (ENUM('Instagram','Facebook','GoogleShopping'))\n   - status (ENUM('DRAFT','SCHEDULED','RUNNING','COMPLETED'))\n   - start_time (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n   - end_time (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n   - creative_file (VARCHAR(255), NOT NULL)\n   - target_audience (JSON)\n   - created_at (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n- **Table: Audits**\n   - audit_id (UUID, PK)\n   - user_id (UUID, FK)\n   - action (VARCHAR(50), NOT NULL)\n   - target_table (VARCHAR(50))\n   - target_id (UUID)\n   - payload (JSON)\n   - timestamp (TIMESTAMP, NOT NULL)\n\n\n#### 📐 [Asset 3: System Architecture Blueprint]\n# GLOBAL PROJECT CONTEXT: autosellhub-automation\n\n## 1. Executive Summary & Tech Stack Blueprint\nThe autosellhub-automation project is a multi-tenant e-commerce automation platform that utilizes a microservices architecture. The tech stack consists of Node.js, Express.js, MongoDB, and Docker. The project will be built using a modular approach, with each module representing a separate microservice. The platform will provide features such as AI-powered pricing suggestions, real-time inventory synchronization, and multi-channel advertising.\n\n## 2. Global Guardrails & Enterprise Compliance Standards\n- **Absolute Workspace Boundary Rule:** The true repository workspace root is permanently fixed at the project root `./`.\n- **Mandatory Path Subdirectory Rule:** Every single file path, configuration, script, diagram, or test asset generated across all prompts MUST be strictly placed inside the `./sources/` directory.\n- **Conditional Path Prefixing:** \n  * All Backend service logics, microservices, configurations, database schemas, and backend tests must be prefixed with: `./sources/backend/`.\n  * All Frontend user interfaces, responsive views, mobile apps, state management packages, and client-side tests must be prefixed with: `./sources/frontend/`.\n- **Java Enterprise Package Standard:** Not applicable, as the project uses Node.js.\n- **Strict Package-to-Path Mapping:** Not applicable, as the project uses Node.js.\n- **Strict Tester Target Path Syntax:** Any component targeted by a Tester Sub-Agent must be structured as a strict semi-colon separated pair `<source_component_or_token>;<test_suite_file_to_execute>`.\n- **Memory, Ingestion, & Loop Constraints:** All generated code structures must strictly avoid runtime in-memory large dataset loops.\n\n## 3. USER PROMPT (Dynamic Task Execution Pattern)\n\n### 📥 Input Documents Payload\nBelow are the live engineering assets under evaluation:\n\n#### 💡 [Asset 1: Core Product Idea]\n# AutoSellHub - công cụ tự động hóa cửa hàng thương mại điện tử\n\n- **Bài toán được giải quyết:** Chủ cửa hàng thương mại điện tử nhỏ thiếu thời gian để quản lý giá cả, tồn kho và chiến dịch quảng cáo trên nhiều kênh bán hàng.\n- **Giải pháp & Quy trình:** AutoSellHub là nền tảng không mã, sử dụng AI để tự động điều chỉnh giá, đồng bộ tồn kho theo thời gian thực và lên lịch đăng quảng cáo trên Instagram, Facebook và Google Shopping thông qua giao diện kéo thả đơn giản.\n- **Đối tượng mục tiêu:** Chủ shop thương mại điện tử mới, cửa hàng bán lẻ trực tuyến quy mô nhỏ và các startup thương mại điện tử.\n- **Điểm khác biệt độc đáo (USP):** AI tối ưu giá cả dựa trên cạnh tranh và nhu cầu, khả năng tích hợp đa kênh mà không cần lập trình, giúp người dùng tăng doanh số chỉ trong vài ngày.\n\n#### 📄 [Asset 2: Software Requirement Specification (SRS)]\n## 1. PROJECT OVERVIEW\n- Product Objectives & Core Values\n   - Tối ưu hoá giá bán dựa trên AI, đồng bộ tồn kho thời gian thực, lên lịch quảng cáo đa kênh.\n   - Đảm bảo sự đơn giản, hiệu quả, bảo mật.\n- Target User Personas\n   - Chủ shop nhỏ (1-10 SKU), người mới bắt đầu, startup thương mại điện tử.\n- Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) Matrix\n   - Admin: Quản lý hệ thống, cấu hình kênh, xem báo cáo.\n   - Merchant: Quản lý sản phẩm, giá, chiến dịch, xem KPI.\n   - Analyst: Xem báo cáo chi tiết, xuất dữ liệu.\n\n## 2. FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS\n**Epic Module: Autonomous Pricing Engine**\n- Feature: AI Pricing Suggestion\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to receive daily suggested price ranges for each SKU so that I can stay competitive.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the system has been seeded with competitor data for the last 7 days, when the pricing engine runs at 03:00 AM, then the system should generate price suggestions within 30 seconds and store them in the database.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - SKU_ID (string, not null, uuid), Current_Price (decimal, >=0), Competitor_Prices (array of decimal, length >=1), Demand_Score (decimal, 0-1).\n\n**Epic Module: Inventory Sync**\n- Feature: Real-Time Stock Sync\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want the inventory levels to sync automatically from my ERP/API so that I never oversell.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the ERP pushes a stock update payload, when the webhook is received, then the system updates the SKU stock within 5 seconds and logs the change.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - SKU_ID, Stock_Quantity (int, >=0), Updated_At (datetime, UTC).\n\n**Epic Module: Advertising Scheduler**\n- Feature: Drag‑and‑Drop Campaign Builder\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to drag and drop ad creatives into a timeline so that I can schedule posts across Instagram, Facebook, and Google Shopping.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given a campaign timeline layout, when the user drops an image, then the system validates the image size (<5MB) and schedule time (>=current time), stores the plan, and triggers API calls at scheduled time.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - Campaign_ID, Platform (enum), Creative_File (image/jpeg/png), Start_Time, End_Time, Target_Audience (json).\n\n**Epic Module: User Management**\n- Feature: OAuth2 + MFA\n   - User Story: As an Admin I want to enforce MFA for all users so that the system remains secure.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given a user in state 'PENDING_MFA', when the user completes MFA, then the account is set to ACTIVE and access token is issued.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - User_ID Mik, Email, Password (hashed, bcrypt), Phone_Number (E.164), MFA_Method (sms/email).\n\n**Epic Module: Analytics Dashboard**\n- Feature: KPI Reporting\n   - User Story: As a Merchant I want to view daily sales, conversion, and ROI metrics so that I can adjust strategies.\n   - Acceptance Criteria:\n     Given the last 30 days of data, when the dashboard loads, then it displays charts within 2 seconds and allows export to CSV.\n   - Data Inputs & Field Validations:\n     - Sale_Amount (decimal), Order_Count (int), Campaign_ID, Date.\n\n## 3. EXCEPTION FLOWS & EDGE CASES\n- Network & Connectivity Drops\n   - The system retries failed API calls up to 3 times with exponential backoff (1s, 2s, 4s). If still failing, queues the request in a durable queue for later.\n- Invalid Inputs & Concurrency Issues\n   - All inputs are validated server‑side; duplicate SKU updates are serialized via optimistic locking (version field). On conflict, return 409 Conflict.\n- System Recovery & Error Notifications\n   - Errors trigger alerts to DevOps via Slack & email; the system logs are stored in a central audit log.\n\n## 4. NON-FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS\n- Performance Metrics\n   - API response time <= 200 ms for 95% of requests.\n   - Pricing engine recomputes all SKUs within 5 minutes.\n- Security\n   - Data at rest encrypted with AES-256.\n   - Token based auth (JWT) with 24h expiry, refresh tokens 30 days.\n   - OWASP Top 10 controls: XSS, CSRF, Injection mitigated.\n- Scalability & Availability\n   - Auto‑scaling groups with min 2 nodes, max 10.\n   - 99.9% uptime SLA, graceful failover across 2 AZs.\n\n## 5. System Overview & Scope\n\n### 1.1 System Purpose\nAutoSellHub is an enterprise-grade, no‑code automation platform that enables small e‑commerce merchants to (1) dynamically optimize product pricing using AI, (2) synchronize inventory across sales channels in real time, and (3) schedule cross‑channel advertising campaigns (Instagram, Facebook, Google Shopping) via a drag‑and‑drop UI. The system is built to be multi‑tenant, highly available, and fully auditable, delivering measurable revenue uplift within the first 30\u202fdays of merchant onboarding.\n\n### 1.2 Core Scope (IN‑SCOPE)\n- AI‑driven pricing engine that consumes competitor data, demand signals, and margin rules.\n- Real‑time inventory webhook ingestion from ERP/CRM systems with optimistic locking.\n- Drag‑and‑drop advertising campaign builder supporting Instagram, Facebook, and Google Shopping.\n- No‑code UI layer providing form builders, rule editors, and visual workflow designer.\n- Multi‑tenant architecture with data isolation, role‑based access control (RBAC), and audit logging.\n- Analytics dashboard displaying sales, pricing effectiveness, inventory health, and ad performance KPIs.\n- End‑to‑end security controls: JWT authentication (24\u202fh expiry), MFA, AES‑256 at‑rest, TLS\u202f1.3, OWASP Top\u202f10 mitigations.\n- Exception handling with durable message queues, exponential back‑off retry, and dead‑letter queues.\n- CI/CD pipelines, automated testing, and observability stack (Prometheus + Grafana, ELK).\n- Phase‑based rollout (User Management → Pricing & Inventory → Scheduler → Multi‑Tenancy → Production).\n\n### 1.3 Out‑of‑Scope\n- Full ERP integration beyond the defined inventory webhook contract.\n- Native ad‑account management (e.g., creating or updating ad accounts on external platforms).\n- Custom UI theming or branding beyond the no‑code builder.\n- Advanced machine‑learning model training; only inference and rule application are required.\n- Physical hardware provisioning; cloud‑only deployment is assumed.\n\n## ⚙️ 2. Functional Requirements & Technical Specifications\n\n### 🔹 Module: User Management & Authentication (UM)\n\n- **FR‑UM‑01: Merchant Sign‑up**\n  - **Description**: A prospective merchant accesses the public registration form, provides email, password, and company name. The system validates uniqueness of email, enforces password complexity (minimum 12 characters, upper/lower/digit/special), and triggers an email verification link.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Form validation latency ≤\u202f150\u202fms; email verification link expires after 48\u202fh; password hashed with Argon2id (salt length 16\u202fB, memory 64\u202fMiB, iterations 3).\n  - **Exception Flow**: Duplicate email → returns HTTP\u202f409 with error code ERR‑UM‑001; malformed input → HTTP\u202f400 with ERR‑UM‑002; SMTP failure → retry up to 3 attempts with exponential back‑off, then logs incident and notifies admin.\n\n- **FR‑UM‑02: Merchant Login & MFA**\n  - **Description**: Authenticated user supplies email and password, receives a one‑time password (OTP) via SMS or authenticator app, and submits the code to complete login.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Authentication latency ≤\u202f200\u202fms; OTP generation and delivery ≤\u202f5\u202fs; session JWT token validity 24\u202fh; refresh token validity 30\u202fd.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Invalid credentials → HTTP\u202f401 ERR‑UM‑003; MFA failure (expired/invalid code) → HTTP\u202f403 ERR‑UM‑004; rate‑limit exceeded (5 attempts/15\u202fmin) → HTTP\u202f429 ERR‑UM‑005.\n\n- **FR‑UM‑03: Session Management**\n  - **Description**: Upon successful MFA, the system issues a signed JWT containing user_id, tenant_id, role, and expiry timestamp. Refresh endpoint issues a new JWT using a secure refresh token stored in the user’s secure token vault.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Token signing time ≤\u202f10\u202fms; token revocation on logout or admin action; token rotation on privilege change.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Invalid refresh token → HTTP\u202f401 ERR‑UM‑006; token already revoked → HTTP\u202f409 ERR‑UM‑007.\n\n### 🔹 Module: Tenant & Multi‑tenancy Management (TM)\n\n- **FR‑TM‑01: Tenant Provisioning**\n  - **Description**: After merchant sign‑up, a tenant record is created with unique tenant_id, default schema, and initial subscription tier. The tenant’s default database schema is provisioned in the shared‑infrastructure cluster.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Tenant creation time ≤\u202f2\u202fs; tenant_id UUID v4; schema version 1.0.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Duplicate tenant name → HTTP\u202f409 ERR‑TM‑001; provisioning failure → retry up to 2×, then alert ops team.\n\n- **FR‑TM‑02: Data Isolation Enforcement**\n  - **Description**: All CRUD operations on tenant‑specific data are filtered by tenant_id at the service layer and enforced via row‑level security in the database.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Query execution ≤\u202f100\u202fms; isolation compliance validated via automated security scans weekly.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Access attempt to foreign tenant data → HTTP\u202f403 ERR‑TM‑002; security scan failure → triggers incident ticket.\n\n- **FR‑TM‑03: Tenant Deactivation & Data Retention**\n  - **Description**: Admin can deactivate a tenant, which revokes all tokens, locks the account, and schedules data purge after 30\u202fdays (retention for audit logs).\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Deactivation latency ≤\u202f5\u202fs; data purge job runs nightly; audit logs retained 365\u202fdays per GDPR.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Purge job failure → retry every 10\u202fmin for 72\u202fh, then alert; concurrent access during purge → queued and retried.\n\n### 🔹 Module: AI Pricing Engine (PE)\n\n- **FR‑PE‑01: Pricing Suggestion Generation**\n  - **Description**: For a given product, the engine fetches competitor prices via API, computes a demand score from recent sales velocity, applies margin rules, and returns an optimal price suggestion within 30\u202fseconds.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: End‑to‑end suggestion latency ≤\u202f30\u202fs; competitor API call timeout 5\u202fs; demand score range 0‑100; price suggestion precision 2 decimal places.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Competitor API unavailable → fallback to static benchmark price; demand data missing → default score 50; engine timeout → return cached suggestion with “stale” flag; retry after 5\u202fmin.\n\n- **FR‑PE‑02: Pricing Rule Management**\n  - **Description**: Merchants can define pricing rules (e.g., markup %, discount thresholds) via the no‑code UI; rules are stored as JSON schemas and applied during suggestion generation.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Rule creation latency ≤\u202f500\u202fms; rule validation against schema ≤\u202f100\u202fms; rule set size ≤\u202f50 per tenant.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Invalid JSON → HTTP\u202f400 ERR‑PE‑001; duplicate rule name → HTTP\u202f409 ERR‑PE‑002.\n\n- **FR‑PE‑03: Pricing Application & Audit**\n  - **Description**: When a merchant accepts a suggestion, the system updates the product price, records the change in the audit log with before/after values, user_id, and timestamp.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Price update latency ≤\u202f200\u202fms; audit entry size ≤\u202f2\u202fKB; audit log write throughput 10\u202fk entries/min.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Concurrent price update → optimistic locking failure → HTTP\u202f409 ERR‑PE‑003; audit write failure → retry 3×, then log to dead‑letter queue.\n\n### 🔹 Module: Real‑time Inventory Sync (IS)\n\n- **FR‑IS‑01: Inventory Webhook Ingestion**\n  - **Description**: External ERP pushes inventory delta events via HTTPS POST to `/webhooks/inventory`. Each payload contains `product_id`, `quantity`, `version` (optimistic lock), and `timestamp`.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Webhook processing latency ≤\u202f5\u202fs; payload validation ≤\u202f200\u202fms; request rate ≤\u202f1000 events/min per tenant.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Invalid schema → HTTP\u202f400 ERR‑IS‑001; version mismatch → HTTP\u202f409 ERR‑IS‑002 (conflict); network timeout → retry with exponential back‑off (max 3 attempts); malformed JSON → dead‑letter queue.\n\n- **FR‑IS‑02: Inventory State Update**\n  - **Description**: Upon validation, the service updates the `Products` collection, incrementing `stock_quantity` and incrementing `version`. If version mismatch, the request is rejected and a conflict response is returned.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Update operation ≤\u202f100\u202fms; row‑level lock wait ≤\u202f50\u202fms; concurrent update detection ≤\u202f10\u202fms.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Database deadlock → retry with backoff; out‑of‑stock threshold (<0) → auto‑set status to “OUT_OF_STOCK”.\n\n- **FR‑IS‑03: Real‑time Stock Query**\n  - **Description**: Merchant UI queries current stock via REST GET `/products/{product_id}/stock`. Returns `quantity`, `status`, and `last_updated`.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Query response ≤\u202f150\u202fms; cache TTL 30\u202fs; cache hit ratio ≥\u202f80\u202f%.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Product not found → HTTP\u202f404 ERR‑IS‑003; cache miss → DB read; DB read failure → fallback to stale cache (max 5\u202fmin).\n\n### 🔹 Module: Multi‑channel Advertising Scheduler (AS)\n\n- **FR‑AS‑01: Campaign Creation via Drag‑and‑Drop Builder**\n  - **Description**: Merchant uses a visual canvas to add ad sets, select target audiences, upload creative (max 5\u202fMB, supported formats: JPG, PNG, MP4), set budget, and define schedule (start/end datetime).\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: UI render latency ≤\u202f300\u202fms; image validation ≤\u202f200\u202fms; file size limit 5\u202fMB; schedule validation ensures start ≥\u202fnow and end ≤\u202f365\u202fdays from start.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Unsupported file type → HTTP\u202f415 ERR‑AS‑001; file size exceeded → HTTP\u202f413 ERR‑AS‑002; invalid schedule → HTTP\u202f400 ERR‑AS‑003.\n\n- **FR‑AS‑001: Campaign Scheduling Service**\n  - **Description**: At the defined start time, the system publishes a scheduled job to the message broker, which triggers external ad‑platform APIs (Instagram, Facebook, Google Shopping) to create or update ads.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Job dispatch latency ≤\u202f100\u202fms; external API call timeout 10\u202fs; retry policy: 3 attempts with 5‑second intervals; SLA for campaign activation ≤\u202f2\u202fmin.\n  - **Exception Flow**: External API authentication failure → HTTP\u202f502 ERR‑AS‑004; quota exceeded → HTTP\u202f429 ERR‑AS‑005; job execution failure → move to dead‑letter queue for manual review.\n\n- **FR‑AS‑02: Campaign Status Monitoring**\n  - **Description**: Real‑time status endpoint `/campaigns/{campaign_id}` returns state (DRAFT, SCHEDULED, RUNNING, COMPLETED, CANCELLED) and performance metrics (impressions, clicks, spend).\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Status query ≤\u202f100\u202fms; status updates pushed via WebSocket to authenticated merchant ≤\u202f200\u202fms; performance metrics refreshed every 5\u202fmin.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Campaign not found → HTTP\u202f404 ERR‑AS‑006; WebSocket disconnect → fallback to polling.\n\n### 🔹 Module: No‑code UI Builder (UI)\n\n- **FR‑UI‑01: Visual Rule Editor**\n  - **Description**: Drag‑and‑drop rule builder allows merchants to construct pricing or inventory rules using a library of condition blocks (e.g., “if competitor price < X”, “if stock < Y”) and action blocks (e.g., “set price to Z”, “trigger reorder”).\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Rule composition latency ≤\u202f500\u202fms; rule serialization size ≤\u202f10\u202fKB; UI state persistence via local storage (5\u202fMB limit).\n  - **Exception Flow**: Invalid rule syntax → client‑side validation error ERR‑UI‑001; save failure → HTTP\u202f503 ERR‑UI‑002 with retry.\n\n- **FR‑UI‑02: Theme & Branding Customization**\n  - **Description**: Merchants can select pre‑defined color palettes and fonts; customizations are stored as tenant‑specific CSS variables.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Theme application ≤\u202f50\u202fms; palette storage ≤\u202f1\u202fKB per tenant.\n  - **Exception Flow**: CSS parsing error → fallback to default theme; storage quota exceeded → HTTP\u202f507 ERR‑UI‑003.\n\n### 🔹 Module: Analytics & Reporting (AR)\n\n- **FR‑AR‑01: KPI Dashboard**\n  - **Description**: Consolidated view displaying total sales, average discount applied, inventory turnover, ad spend vs. ROI, and pricing effectiveness (price change impact).\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Dashboard load ≤\u202f2\u202fs; data refresh interval 15\u202fmin; chart rendering ≤\u202f500\u202fms; data accuracy ±1\u202f% vs. source systems.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Data source unavailable → display last‑known values; refresh failure → retry 2×, then show error banner.\n\n- **FR‑AR‑02: Export Reports**\n  - **Description**: Merchants can export dashboards as CSV, PDF, or Excel for periods up to 12\u202fmonths.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Export generation ≤\u202f30\u202fs for 10\u202fk rows; PDF generation ≤\u202f5\u202fs; file size limit 50\u202fMB.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Export timeout → HTTP\u202f504 ERR‑AR‑001; unsupported format → HTTP\u202f415 ERR‑AR‑002.\n\n### 🔹 Module: System Integration & API Gateway (IG)\n\n- **FR‑IG‑01: Unified API Gateway**\n  - **Description**: All internal and external services are exposed via Kong/Apigee API Gateway with rate limiting, request validation, and authentication.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Request routing latency ≤\u202f50\u202fms; rate limit 1000 requests/min per tenant; request size limit 10\u202fMB.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Rate limit breach → HTTP\u202f429 ERR‑IG‑001; malformed request → HTTP\u202f400 ERR‑IG‑002.\n\n- **FR‑IG‑02: External Platform Connectors**\n  - **Description**: Connectors for Instagram Graph API, Facebook Ads API, and Google Shopping API are implemented as micro‑services behind the gateway.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Connector call latency ≤\u202f2\u202fs; retry policy 3 attempts with 1‑second backoff; connector health check every 30\u202fs.\n  - **Exception Flow**: OAuth token expired → automatic refresh using stored refresh token; API version mismatch → fallback to latest stable version.\n\n### 🔹 Module: Security & Audit (SA)\n\n- **FR‑SA‑01: Data Encryption**\n  - **Description**: All data at rest is encrypted with AES‑256 using keys managed by AWS KMS; in‑transit traffic uses TLS\u202f1.3 with forward secrecy.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Encryption overhead ≤\u202f5\u202f%; key rotation every 90\u202fdays; TLS handshake ≤\u202f100\u202fms.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Key management service outage → fallback to local HSM; TLS handshake failure → abort connection.\n\n- **FR‑SA‑02: Comprehensive Audit Logging**\n  - **Description**: Every critical action (user login, price change, inventory update, campaign creation) is logged to an immutable audit collection. Log entry includes `audit_id`, `timestamp`, `tenant_id`, `user_id`, `action`, `resource`, `old_value`, `new_value`, `source_ip`, `user_agent`, `session_id`.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Log write latency ≤\u202f200\u202fms; log retention 365\u202fdays; log storage efficiency ≥\u202f90\u202f% compressed.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Audit write failure → retry 3×, then write to dead‑letter queue; log corruption detection → trigger alert and initiate log re‑ingestion.\n\n- **FR‑SA‑03: OWASP Controls**\n  - **Description**: Implemented input validation, output encoding, SQL injection prevention, XSS mitigation, and rate limiting as per OWASP Top\u202f10.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Security scan passes with zero critical findings; vulnerability remediation SLA 48\u202fh.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Scan detection of new vulnerability → auto‑ quarantine affected endpoint; manual review required.\n\n### 🔹 Module: Exception Handling & Retry (EH)\n\n- **FR‑EH‑01: Durable Message Queue for Failed Operations**\n  - **Description**: Failed API calls (e.g., ad‑platform creation) are published to RabbitMQ with persistent delivery. Consumer processes messages with exponential back‑off (base 1\u202fs, max 30\u202fs) and dead‑letter routing after 5 attempts.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Queue depth monitoring ≤\u202f10\u202fk messages; message persistence latency ≤\u202f100\u202fms; dead‑letter processing latency ≤\u202f5\u202fs.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Consumer crash → automatic failover to standby consumer; message TTL expires → move to dead‑letter exchange.\n\n- **FR‑EH‑02: Circuit Breaker Pattern**\n  - **Description**: For external dependencies (competitor price API, ad platforms), a Hystrix‑style circuit breaker trips after 5 consecutive failures within 60\u202fs, opening for 30\u202fs before attempting again.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Circuit state changes logged; fallback response (stale data) returned during open state.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Circuit trips → HTTP\u202f503 ERR‑EH‑001; manual admin intervention resets circuit.\n\n### 🔹 Module: Deployment & Observability (DO)\n\n- **FR‑DO‑01: CI/CD Pipeline**\n  - **Description**: GitOps workflow using ArgoCD deploys container images to Kubernetes clusters across 2 availability zones. Automated unit, integration, and security tests run before promotion to production.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Build time ≤\u202f10\u202fmin; test suite execution ≤\u202f15\u202fmin; deployment rollout ≤\u202f5\u202fmin; rollback time ≤\u202f2\u202fmin.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Test failure → block promotion; deployment health check failure → auto‑rollback.\n\n- **FR‑DO‑02: Observability Stack**\n  - **Description**: Prometheus collects metrics (CPU, memory, request latency, error rate); Grafana renders dashboards; ELK stack aggregates logs; Jaeger provides distributed tracing.\n  - **Metrics/Thresholds**: Metric collection interval 15\u202fs; alert latency ≤\u202f30\u202fs; trace sampling rate 1\u202f%; log indexing latency ≤\u202f5\u202fs.\n  - **Exception Flow**: Metric collector down → fallback to cloudwatch; trace system overload → reduce sampling to 0.5\u202f%.\n\n## 🛡️ 3. Non-Functional Requirements (NFR)\n\n- **NFR‑1: Performance & Scalability**\n  - **Throughput**: System must sustain 10\u202f000 concurrent authenticated users with an average request rate of 5\u202f000 RPS.\n  - **Latency**: API response time ≤\u202f200\u202fms for 95th percentile; AI pricing suggestion ≤\u202f30\u202fs; inventory webhook processing ≤\u202f5\u202fs.\n  - **Scalability**: Auto‑scaling groups adjust pods based on CPU >\u202f70\u202f% or request latency >\u202f200\u202fms; horizontal pod autoscaler maintains ≤\u202f200\u202fms latency under load.\n  - **Data Volume**: Support up to 10\u202fmillion product SKUs per tenant; audit log growth ≤\u202f5\u202fGB/day.\n\n- **NFR‑2: Security & Compliance**\n  - **Authentication**: JWT with RSA‑2048 signature, 24\u202fh expiry, MFA required for all privileged actions.\n  - **Authorization**: Role‑Based Access Control (RBAC) with minimum two roles (Admin, Merchant, Analyst); permissions enforced at API gateway.\n  - **Encryption**: AES‑256 at‑rest (KMS managed); TLS\u202f1.3 for all in‑transit traffic; perfect forward secrecy.\n  - **Password Policy**: Minimum 12 characters, complexity, no reuse of last 12 passwords; hashing Argon2id (salt 16\u202fB, memory 64\u202fMiB, iterations 3).\n  - **Audit**: Immutable logs with SHA‑256 hash chain; retention 365\u202fdays; logs include session_id, source_ip, user_agent.\n  - **Compliance**: GDPR data residency (EU region), PCI‑DSS for payment data (if extended), SOC\u202f2 Type\u202fII audited.\n\n- **NFR‑3: Reliability & Availability**\n  - **Uptime**: 99.99\u202f% annual availability (≈\u202f52.6\u202fmin downtime allowed).\n  - **Recovery**: RTO (Recovery Time Objective) ≤\u202f4\u202fh for any single service failure; RPO (Recovery Point Objective) ≤\u202f5\u202fmin.\n  - **Disaster Recovery**: Active‑active across 2 AWS regions; automated failover via Route\u202f53 health checks.\n  - **Backup**: Daily full backups retained 30\u202fdays; point‑in‑time recovery (PITR) enabled for databases.\n  - **Monitoring**: Health checks every 30\u202fs; alerts for latency >\u202f200\u202fms, error rate >\u202f1\u202f%, queue depth >\u202f5\u202fk.\n\n## ⛓️ 4. Traceability & System Constraints\n\n### 4.1 Business Constraints\n- **Budget**: Cap of $2.5\u202fM for development and initial 12‑month operational costs.\n- **Timeline**: MVP launch within 9\u202fmonths; full feature set within 18\u202fmonths.\n- **Market Entry**: Must be live in target EU market by Q3\u202f2026.\n\n### 4.2 Technical Constraints\n- **Technology Stack**: Node.js v20 (Express), MongoDB 7.0 with sharding, Docker/Kubernetes (EKS), AWS (S3, SQS, SNS, CloudWatch, KMS), RabbitMQ for durable queues, Redis for caching, Prometheus/Grafana/ELK for observability.\n- **Data Schema**: All tenant data stored in separate MongoDB databases; `Products` collection includes fields `product_id`, `tenant_id`, `name`, `price`, `stock_quantity`, `version`, `status` (ACTIVE, OUT_OF_STOCK, DISCONTINUED). `Users` collection includes `user_id`, `tenant_id`, `email`, `password_hash`, `role`, `mfa_enabled`. `Audits` collection includes `audit_id`, `timestamp`, `tenant_id`, `user_id`, `action`, `resource`, `old_value`, `new_value`, `source_ip`, `user_agent`, `session_id`.\n- **API Versioning**: Use URL‑based versioning (`/v1/`) with backward compatibility guarantee for 2 major versions.\n- **Logging Standards**: Structured JSON logs with ISO‑8601 timestamps; log level hierarchy (ERROR, WARN, INFO, DEBUG).\n- **Security Standards**: OWASP Top\u202f10 mitigations, CWE mapping, CVSS scoring for vulnerabilities, quarterly penetration testing.\n\n### 4.3 Traceability Matrix\n\n| FR Identifier | Business Goal (Idea) | Mapping Explanation |\n|---------------|----------------------|---------------------|\n| FR‑UM‑01 | “No‑code UI” – merchants can self‑register and manage shop | Sign‑up flow provides the entry point for merchants to platform” – separate shops | Tenant provisioning isolates each merchant’s data and configuration. |\n| FR‑TM‑02 | “Data isolation” – privacy & compliance | Row‑level security enforces strict tenant boundaries. |\n| FR‑PE‑01 | “AI price optimization” – dynamic pricing | Generates price suggestions based on competition and demand. |\n| FR‑PE‑02 | “AI price optimization” – rule‑based customization | Allows merchants to define pricing rules via no‑code UI. |\n| FR‑PE‑03 | “AI price optimization” – auditability | Records price changes for transparency and compliance. |\n| FR‑IS‑01 | “Real‑time inventory sync” – never oversell | Webhook ingestion ensures stock levels are current across channels. |\n| FR‑IS‑02 | “Real‑time inventory sync” – optimistic locking | Prevents race conditions and campaign builder | Core no‑code UI for creating ad campaigns. |\n| FR‑AS‑001 | “Multi‑channel ad scheduling” – automated execution | Schedules and triggers ads on external platforms. |\n| FR‑AS‑02 | “Multi‑channel ad scheduling” – performance monitoring | Gives merchants real‑time insight into campaign health. |\n| FR‑UI‑01 | “No‑code UI” – visual rule editor | Enables merchants to build FR‑SA‑01 | “Security” – encryption & transport safety | Protects data at rest and in transit. |\n| FR‑SA‑02 | “Security” – audit logging | Provides immutable trail for compliance. |\n| FR‑SA‑03 | Data Dictionary (Core Tables)\n\n| Table | Primary Key | Key Fields (Data Types) | Constraints & Notes |\n|-------|-------------|------------------------|---------------------|\n| **Tenants** | `tenant_id` (UUID) | `name`, `subscription_tier`, `created_at`, `status` (ACTIVE, SUSPENDED, CLOSED) | `status` enforces soft delete; `subscription_tier` enum. |\n| **Users** | `user_id` (UUID) | `tenant_id`, `email`, `password_hash`, `role` (OWNER, ADMIN, MERCHANT, ANALYST), `mfa_enabled`, `created_at`, `last_login` | `email` unique per tenant; `password_hash` Argon2id; `role` defines RBAC. |\n| **Products** | `product_id` (UUID) | `tenant_id`, `name`, `sku`, `price` (DECIMAL(10,2)), `stock_quantity` (INT), `version` (INT), `status` (ACTIVE, OUT_OF_STOCK, DISCONTINUED), `last_updated` | `version` used for optimistic locking; `status` drives UI availability. |\n| **InventoryEvents** | `event_id` (UUID) | `tenant_id`, `product_id`, `quantity_change`, `new_quantity`, `version`, `event_type` (IN, OUT, ADJUST), `timestamp`, `source` (ERP_WEBHOOK, MANUAL) | `version` ensures idempotency; `timestamp` indexed for real‑time queries. |\n| **Campaigns** | `campaign_id` (UUID) | `tenant_id`, `name`, `ad_platform` (INSTAGRAM, FACEBOOK, GOOGLE_SHOPPING), `status` (DRAFT, SCHEDULED, RUNNING, COMPLETED, CANCELLED), `schedule_start`, `schedule_end`, `budget`, `created_by`, `created_at` | `status` drives workflow; `schedule_start` must be ≥ now. |\n| **CreativeAssets** | `asset_id` (UUID) | `tenant_id`, `campaign_id`, `file_name`, `file_path`, `file_size` (BIGINT), `mime_type`, `uploaded_at` | `file_size` ≤\u202f5\u202fMB; `mime_type` validated against allowed list. |\n| **PricingRules** | `rule_id` (UUID) | `tenant_id`, `rule_name`, `conditions` (JSON), `actions` (JSON), `enabled`, `created_at` | `conditions` and `actions` stored as JSON schema; max 50 rules per tenant. |\n| **AuditLogs** | `audit_id` (UUID) | `timestamp`, `tenant_id`, `user_id`, `session_id`, `action`, `resource`, `old_value` (JSON), `new_value` (JSON), `source_ip`, `user_agent`, `request_id` | Immutable append‑only; indexed on `tenant_id`, `timestamp`, `resource`. |\n| **Sessions** | `session_id` (UUID) | `user_id`, `tenant_id`, `jwt_token`, `refresh_token`, `issued_at`, `expires_at`, `revoked` | `revoked` flag for logout; tokens rotated on privilege change. |\n| **DeadLetterQueue** | `dlq_id` (UUID) | `original_message_id`, `exchange`, `routing_key`, `payload`, `error`, `retry_count`, `first_failed`, `last_attempt` | Retained 30\u202fdays; manual admin review required. |\n\n## 📄 6. Glossary (selected)\n\n| Term | Definition |\n|------|------------|\n| **AI Pricing Engine** | Micro‑service that consumes competitor price feeds, demand signals, and merchant‑defined rules to generate optimal price suggestions. |\n| **Optimistic Locking** | Concurrency control technique using a `version` field to detect and resolve conflicts without database locks. |\n| **No‑code UI** | Visual development environment allowing merchants to construct business rules and campaigns without writing code. |\n| **Multi‑Tenant** | Architectural pattern where a single application instance serves multiple isolated customers (merchants) with data segregation. |\n| **Webhook** | HTTP POST callback from external ERP to `/webhooks/inventory` delivering inventory delta events. |\n| **Circuit Breaker** | Failure‑handling pattern that temporarily blocks calls to a failing external service to prevent cascade failures. |\n| **Dead‑Letter Queue** | Secondary queue for messages that cannot be processed after all retry attempts. |\n| **RTO / RPO** | Recovery Time Objective (≤\u202f4\u202fh) and Recovery Point Objective (≤\u202f5\u202fmin) for disaster recovery. |\n| **OWASP** | Open Web Application Security Project; top‑10 web application security risks and mitigation controls. |\n\n--- \n\n*End of SRS Document.*', pydantic=None, json_dict=None, agent='Enterprise Business Analyst', output_format=<OutputFormat.RAW: 'raw'>, messages=[{'role': 'system', 'content': 'You are Enterprise Business Analyst. # 📋 Agent Specification: Enterprise Business Analyst (BA)\n\n## 📌 1. SYSTEM PROMPT (Agent Core Configuration)\n\n### 🎭 Role & Identity\n- **Role Name**: Enterprise Business Analyst\n- **Persona**: A meticulous, seasoned Senior Business Analyst with 15+ years of experience authoring and overhauling Software Requirements Specifications (SRS) for Fortune-500 enterprises. You translate ambiguous business visions into watertight, verifiable, and highly structured functional specifications.
